The mechanism bounds it: `seeded_causes` is the ONLY input\nthat differs between the old walkers and the new derivations, and it is nonzero\nonly on a seeded std leaf.\n\n## 4. Conformance rows (obligation 3) — cause × context, K111-K115\n\nOne row per CAUSE, each stating that cause's answer in all four contexts (sync\nbody, thread body, main, framed body), with a covering file per cell.\n\n- **K111** `cooperative` — new `K111_suspension_causes_decide_framing.saw` (the\n  framed-body column for every cause × every callee shape: free function,\n  method, generic instance, imported public + module-private helper, the test\n  intrinsic, and a `no`-cause control, with EXPECT-IR-CONTAINS/ABSENT per cell),\n  + `errors/copy_policy_hook_suspends_in_sync_fn.saw` + K83 + `coro_conservative_free_callee_not_framed.saw`.\n- **K112** `blocking` — `errors/sync_blocking_extern.saw` + K84's two files +\n  `expr_suspend_blocking.saw`; the row records finding (d).\n- **K113** `test_suspend` — new\n  `K113_test_suspend_is_a_frame_boundary_not_an_executor.saw` (framed at the\n  drive root, `__Frame_main` ABSENT for the same intrinsic in `main`) +\n  `errors/sync_direct_suspend.saw` + K114 + K111.\n- **K114** `closure_call` — new\n  `K114_thread_body_refuses_every_cause_but_blocking.saw` (the two thread-body\n  cells K83/K84 left unmeasured: `test_suspend` and `closure_call`) +\n  `errors/sync_context_calls_a_closure_taking_helper.saw` + K111 + K105's files.\n- **K115** `existential_dispatch` — `errors/any_sync_suspend.saw` + the SL-323\n  XFAIL pin.\n\nINDEX header recounted: **536 rows, 281 carry a file here, 255 covered\nelsewhere** (was 531/278/253). The pre-existing duplicate K53 row untouched.\n\n## 5. Gates\n\n- **Per-commit gate** and **TERMINAL battery**: `tools/battery.sh` full,\n  **28 stages GREEN in 3409s**, including `suite` (**2565 passed, 12 xfailed**,\n  zero XPASS, zero uncited), `reemit`, `irdet --all`, `gmgate`, `bootstrap`,\n  `corodiff`, and `freestanding` (**36 passed across riscv32 + arm64**). Log:\n  `.build/scratch/battery.log`. Run under the machine-wide lock\n  (`/private/tmp/claude-501/saw-suite-lock`), acquired and released around it.\n- The 12 xfails are the 10 pre-existing ones plus my two cited pins (SL-323,\n  SL-326). `citations`: 13 citations, 12 xfail + 1 ledger, 0 stale.\n\n## 6. Adversarial self-check — and a fifth finding\n\nProbe set beyond the tests, compiled AND run, `.build/scratch/`:\n\n- **two-module + same-name**: `adv_a`/`adv_b` declaring the same free-function\n  names (`worker`, `_shared_name`, `through_generic`) at OPPOSITE causes, and\n  `adv_c`/`adv_d` declaring the same really-suspending generic `warp`.\n- **generic-via-helper**, **closure-caller**, **imported public vs\n  module-private**: all in `K111` with per-cell IR assertions — `__Frame_really_free`,\n  `__Frame_boundary`, `__Frame_Widget_tick`, `__Frame_hop$m$$1$Int`,\n  `__Frame_yield_public$m$sl306mod`, `__Frame_yield_via_private$m$sl306mod` and\n  `__Frame__yield_private$m$sl306mod` PRESENT; `__Frame_closure_only_free`,\n  `__Frame_plain_free`, `__Frame_Widget_fold`, `__Frame_pass`,\n  `__Frame_fold_public`, `__Frame__fold_private` ABSENT. Identical frame set on\n  main and on U4.\n- **IR byte-compare, main vs U4**: 27 programs (the SL-280 rows, the SL-306\n  matrix, the design-44 nested-suspend rows, the json/module rows, the blocking\n  row) — `MISMATCHES: none`.\n\n**SL-326 (filed high) — the finding the adversarial set turned up.** A suspending\nGENERIC free call in an EXPRESSION position inside a driven body is hoisted by\ndesign 120's ANF and never PROMOTED, so the instantiation has no frame and the\nhoisted call is a plain call to a suspending body inside the synthesized resume:\n``cannot suspend in `sync func` method: method `__Frame_main.resume` calls\n`warp$1$Int` → yield_now``. The statement-level `let` cell is the control and\ncompiles. Position matrix probed: interpolation, argument and binary operand all\nfail; entry-module and imported alike; qualifier and selective-import spellings\nalike. Mechanism: `_promote_nested_generic_calls` walks the positions design 74\nshape 3 was written against while design 120/224 widened the HOIST's positions —\ndiscovery and lowering disagreeing on a position set, which is design 275's own\ndiagnosis one site over, and U2's shape table is the artifact that catches it.\nPre-existing on main; pinned XFAIL as\n`examples/coro_generic_call_in_an_expression_position.saw`.\n\n## 7. Out of scope, untouched\n\nNo ledger, no shape table, no new splits; the state machine, the seven split\nroutines, the drive loop, the executor and design 44's frame embedding are\nunchanged. SL-215 / SL-317 / SL-287 / SL-316 not touched and their pins did not\nflip. Nothing closed in the tracker; `designs/todo.md`'s design-275 entry marked\nin place.\n\n## 8. Filed this unit\n\nSL-323 (existential_dispatch framed), SL-324 (suspending parameter default),\nSL-325 (design 260's consuming fences), SL-326 (generic call in an expression\nposition, high).\n","created":"1789774386","id":"c14"},{"author":"agent:claude-sl318u4","body":"# U4 addendum — the two review fixes, regated (final tip `672d20dc`)\n\n## FIX 1 — the placeholder, and its provenance\n\nThe refusal printed ``closure calls <suspension source>``. Provenance matches\nthe lead's: NOT general to seeded leaves and NOT pre-existing. A seeded std leaf\ncarried ONE representative source label; the thread-body refusal STRIKES the\nblocking cause (design 242 ruling 9 permits it), so for a method whose\nrepresentative label is the blocking one and whose refusal is satisfied by the\nCOOPERATIVE cause — `Command.output` — the path walk found nothing left to name\nat the leaf and fell to `_effect_path`'s robustness fallback. `Channel.receive`\n(one cause) renders correctly on main and on the branch alike.\n\nFixed where the seed is minted: `build_builtin_namespace` walks the non-blocking\npath too (the builtin graph is the only place that path exists) and the leaf\ncarries a source PER CAUSE, **representative first**. Consequences, all probed:\n\n- striking reader: ``closure calls `Command.output` → io_wait (`Command.output`\n  suspends at line 494)`` — the method AND the cause that decided it;\n- non-striking reader, byte-identical to main: ``cannot suspend in `sync func`\n  declaration: `sync func run_it` calls `Command.output` → blocking extern\n  `__saw_rt_proc_read_stdout` ``;\n- single-cause control, byte-identical to main: ``closure calls\n  `Channel.receive` → __saw_chan_park``.\n\nTwo names moved into `effects.py` so nobody re-derives them: `cause_of_label`\n(the one label→cause map, now asked by the seed instead of a private prefix\ntest) and `PATH_PLACEHOLDER_LABEL` (so a caller BUILDING a table tests for the\nfallback instead of shipping it into a message).\n\n**The pin moved to its own file** — `examples/conformance/K112_thread_body_refuses_a_multi_cause_std_method.saw`,\nwith `EXPECT-ERROR-CONTAINS: closure calls \\`Command.output\\` → io_wait` and\n`EXPECT-ERROR-ABSENT: <suspension source>`. It cannot be a third cell in K114,\nand why is a finding: **SL-327** (filed low). The seed table's node ids do not\nmatch the entry graph's edges at the FIRST settling (`62190` vs `20761`,\ndumped), so this refusal is only reached at a later one — and a compile stops\nafter a settling that reported errors, so beside another refused cell the\nmessage is never emitted. Pre-existing keying (design 206's), made visible by\nthe new refusal; recorded for U1, which takes the seeded-leaf population over.\nK112's INDEX row carries the whole story; header recounted 281/255 → **282 carry\na file here, 254 elsewhere** (536 rows unchanged).\n\n## FIX 2 — stale docstrings\n\n`coro_transform.py:85-90` and `:11328` and `:11342-11344` rewritten for the\ncause set and the named derivations (`frame_boundary` / `might_suspend`). Grepped\nthe branch for `three-valued`, `closure-only` and backticked `really`: the only\nsurvivor is `effects.py:168`, which names the REJECTED draft as the record of\nwhy the set exists. K111's cell labels and its printed strings now spell the\ncause `closure_call` rather than the old `closure-only`.\n\n## Gates at the final tip (`672d20dc`)\n\n`tools/battery.sh` full, under the machine-wide lock: **28 stages GREEN in\n3389s**. `suite` **2566 passed, 12 xfailed** (the 10 pre-existing + my two cited\npins; zero XPASS, zero uncited), `freestanding` **36 passed across riscv32 +\narm64** — that pair is the per-commit gate — plus `reemit`, `irdet --all`,\n`gmgate`, `bootstrap`, `corodiff`, `fuzz`, `citations`, `astgraft`. Log:\n`.build/scratch/battery2.log`.\n\nThree commits, linear, not submitted: `7dbd81da` (the unit), `cd0462fc` (the\nSL-326 pin), `672d20dc` (these two fixes).\n\nFiled by this unit: SL-323, SL-324, SL-325, SL-326, SL-327.\n","created":"1789778644","id":"c15"},{"author":"agent:claude-sawlang","body":"Submitted patch SL-318.p3 (revision 1): design 275 U4: one suspension analysis — a cause set per node, four walkers deleted, five named derivations; std seed carries the set (Thread-body Command.output refused)","created":"1789778697","id":"c16"},{"author":"agent:claude-sawlang","body":"SUBMITTED SL-318.p3 (lead, Sep 19): design 275 U4 at tip 672d20dc (3 commits squashed), LEAD-VALIDATED. `effects.classify_suspensions` computes ONE cause set per node {cooperative, blocking, test_suspend, existential_dispatch, closure_call, unclassified} by one set-union fixpoint; the four walkers (`suspends`, `really_suspending`, `suspends_ignoring_blocking`, `suspends_ignoring_closure_calls`) are DELETED and their consumers read five named derivations (`might_suspend`, `wraps_main`, `refused_in_thread_body`, `frame_boundary`, `closure_only`) whose docstrings list their consumers; 22 consumer sites tabled. The std seed carries the SET per method, which fixed an unmeasured cell: `Thread.spawn { c.output() }` compiled and RAN on main (Command.output parks cooperatively on the child's exit; the seed's one representative label was the blocking one) and is refused now, naming the method and the primitive (K112, pinned with EXPECT-ERROR-CONTAINS + ABSENT `<suspension source>`). Conformance K111-K115 = cause × context. Findings filed, none absorbed: SL-323 (existential_dispatch is a cause nobody classified; kept in frame_boundary for behaviour preservation, XFAIL), SL-324/SL-325 (two `might` readers whose refusal is about a frame), SL-326 (HIGH, U2's cell: a suspending generic call in an EXPRESSION position is ANF-hoisted but never promoted → plain call in the resume; XFAIL pinned), SL-327 (seed node ids vs entry-graph edges at the first settling — U1 takes seeding over). Walker-equivalence probe: 2,778,002 node comparisons over 2499 programs + blade + libs; every mismatch is the seed fix. Agent gates at the tip: battery 28/28, suite 2566/12x, freestanding 36/36. Lead validation: 17 then 10 filtered rows pass; adversarial probe (same-named parks in two modules, generic via helper, closure-caller, blocking-only, test-suspend-only) frames exactly the expected set; seed probe main-compiles/branch-refuses with the method named; Channel.receive control byte-identical both sides. Behaviour-preserving for the transform: IR byte-compare main vs branch over the SL-280/SL-306/design-44 rows, no mismatches. Does NOT overlap SL-318.p2 (the docs patch touches only the brief; this touches code + todo.md + INDEX).\n","created":"1789778729","id":"c17"},{"author":"agent:sawtracker-tests","body":"Tests passed for SL-318.p3 (revision 1)","created":"1789779179","id":"c18"},{"author":"agent:codex","body":"Approved patch SL-318.p3 (revision 1)\n\nApproved revision 1. No blocking findings.\n\nThe U4 implementation meets the cause-set portion of the amended design: one SCC-safe set-union classifier replaces the separate effect walkers, named derivations preserve the distinctions between author sync checks, thread-body refusal, executor wrapping, and frame boundaries, and generic promotion shares the transform's analysis table. The std-method seed carries the full cause set rather than reducing it to the diagnostic representative. The additional non-blocking diagnostic source makes the newly correct Command.output thread-body refusal actionable.\n\nThe deliberate existential-dispatch framing compatibility is explicit and pinned by SL-323; the generic expression-position limitation is separately pinned by SL-326. Neither is introduced by this refactor. This approves U4, not the remaining ledger, totality, or borrow-lifetime units of SL-318.\n\nIndependent validation on an isolated checkout of base 40230e95 with the exact patch applied:\n- Focused test_runner selection: 18 passed, 2 expected failures (SL-323 and SL-326). Includes K111-K114, K83/K84, ordinary sync/blocking/test-intrinsic refusals, conservative free/method/generic framing and spawned-task regressions, existential refusal, copy-hook refusal, and blocking expression suspension. IR assertions and diagnostic presence/absence checks passed.\n- Independent scratch probe: all 64 cause subsets satisfy the five derivations; all six source classes map correctly; 2,400 node answers over 200 deterministic generated graphs match a separate reachability oracle, including cycles, mixed seeded/direct causes, and missing targets.\n- Inspected the consumer replacements and generic-promotion ordering; searched for remaining legacy walker calls and writes to the derived suspends property.\n\nServer patch tests: passed. Re-fetched and verified revision 1 and its exact diff immediately before recording this approval. No merge performed.\n","created":"1789821979","id":"c19"},{"author":"agent:claude-sawlang","body":"Merged patch SL-318.p3 (revision 1) as a2ef74e1","created":"1789833215","id":"c20"},{"author":"agent:claude-sawlang","body":"Patch SL-318.p2 needs rebase; the base branch moved since it applied. Run patch rebase, or submit a revised diff if it conflicts.","created":"1789833229","id":"c21"},{"author":"agent:claude-sawlang","body":"Revised patch SL-318.p2 to revision 3","created":"1789833267","id":"c22"},{"author":"human:shawn","body":"Approved patch SL-318.p2 (revision 3)","created":"1789833361","id":"c23"},{"author":"agent:claude-sawlang","body":"U4 LANDED (Sep 19): SL-318.p3 r1 codex-approved, server gate green, lead-merged — apply a2ef74e1, merge 668fb0c7. Epic stays OPEN.\n\nWhat landed: `effects.classify_suspensions` computes ONE cause set per node ({cooperative, blocking, test_suspend, closure_call}, SCC-safe fixpoint); the four walkers (`suspends`, `really_suspending`, `suspends_ignoring_blocking`, `suspends_ignoring_closure_calls`) are DELETED and 22 consumers read the five named derivations (`might_suspend` / `wraps_main` / `refused_in_thread_body` / `frame_boundary` / `closure_only`). Std seed table carries the set per method. Behaviour-preserving except the one seed fix: `Command.output` inside a `Thread.spawn { }` body was permitted on main and is refused now (message names the method via per-cause seed sources; pinned on K112/K114). Findings filed along the way: SL-323 (existential_dispatch is a sixth cause; kept in frame_boundary, XFAIL-pinned), SL-324/SL-325 (two `might` readers whose refusal is about a frame), SL-326 (suspending generic call in an EXPRESSION position hoisted but never promoted → plain call in the resume; U2's cell, pinned), SL-327. Battery 28/28 at the tip.\n\nNext: SL-318.p2 (the docs patch, approved r2) bounced needs-rebase under this merge; revised byte-identical as r3 — re-approval + merge. Then dispatch U1 (frame ledger + `corodiscovery` lane).\n","created":"1789833364","id":"c24"},{"author":"agent:sawtracker-tests","body":"Tests passed for SL-318.p2 (revision 3)","created":"1789833782","id":"c25"},{"author":"agent:claude-sawlang","body":"Merged patch SL-318.p2 (revision 3) as 7de4112f","created":"1789833821","id":"c26"},{"author":"agent:claude-sl318u1","body":"# U1 BUILT — ONE discovery ledger (branch `sl318u1`, 4 commits, not submitted)\n\nBuilt on the AMENDED brief (SL-318.p2 r2). PINNED PRE-U1 BASE: branched from\n`668fb0c7`, rebased onto main `8d752a78` when the docs patch landed — `git diff\n668fb0c7 8d752a78 -- sawc/ tools/ test_runner.py` is EMPTY, so the two are one\ncompiler and I used **8d752a78** for the IR byte-compare. Commits, linear:\n`207c7e0f` (conformance rows), `8b3aaaee` (the dump instrument), `b277742b` (the\nledger + consumers), `75e11046` (the lane + flag docs).\n\n## 1. The ledger's read API, and its named consumers\n\n`sawc/coro_ledger.py::FrameLedger` — built once per program by\n`coro_transform._build_frame_ledger` (a worklist to stabilization, extracted out\nof `transform_program`), FROZEN before any body is lowered. A write after the\nfreeze and a MISS at a consumer both raise `LedgerMiss`, which `sawc.py` catches\nbeside `CoroTransformError` and reports through `_report_ice`: design 192 unit\n2's internal-compiler-error line with the key in it. Never a plain call.\n\n`FrameRow(key, kind in {free, method, static, mono-instance}, causes = U4's cause\nSET, boundary, buildable + reason, home_module, splice in {entry, imported, mono})`\nis the callee FACT; `SiteRow(file:line:col, callee, context in {driven root,\nembedded, spawned, closure body, sync body, Thread body}, outcome in {embed,\ninline, refuse(why), declined(why)})` is the call-site DECISION.\n\nCENSUS — one row per consumer, and the same table is the docstring's (obligation\n1). `callee_frame_key`'s sixteen named entry points and the method key's collapse\nto ONE caller: the ledger. `coro_transform.py` no longer imports `frame_keys`.\n\n| consumer | read before | reads now |\n|---|---|---|\n| `_FrameBuilder.__init__` | `callee_frame_key(func)` / `_method_frame_key(struct, name, func.mangled_symbol)` | `key_of` / `method_key_of_decl` |\n| `_FrameBuilder._is_suspension_point` | `callee_frame_key(expr) in self._suspends` | `free_call_frame` |\n| `_FrameBuilder._spans_suspension` | the same membership test | `free_call_frame` |\n| `_FrameBuilder._classify_call` | the same, plus the `callee` it emits | `free_call_frame` |\n| `_FrameBuilder._classify_method_call` | that test + `_suspending_method_target` | `free_call_frame` + `method_target` |\n| `_FrameBuilder._module_free_call_suspends` | `callee_frame_key(mc) in self._suspends` | `free_call_frame` |\n| `_FrameBuilder._method_call_suspends` | `_suspending_method_target(mc, tc)` | `method_target` |\n| `_FrameBuilder._suspending_method_call` | the same | `method_target` |\n| `_FrameBuilder._reject_suspending_method_call` | the same | `method_target` |\n| `_FrameBuilder._reject_buried_suspend_call` | the membership test, `n.module_free_call`, `_suspending_method_target` | `free_call_frame`, `key_of`, `method_target` |\n| `_FrameBuilder._callee_fb` | `self._fbs[info['callee']]` | unchanged — `info['callee']` IS a ledger key now |\n| `_default_expr_suspends` | `nodes[(\"fn\", callee_frame_key(n))].suspends` + `_suspending_method_target` | `might_suspend_free` + `method_target` |\n| `_find_suspending_cycle` | `node.edges` + `t.suspends` | `suspending_edge_targets` |\n| `_analyze_nesting` | `_node_display` + the caller's `is_built` closure over a private set | `node_label` + `is_built` |\n| `_promote_nested_generic_calls` | its OWN `classify_suspensions` walk, `callee_frame_key(fc)`, an `is_mono_instance` store of its own, three separate call rewrites | `instantiation_is_boundary`, `key_of`, `mono_free_bodies`, `rename_call_to`, `register_free_body` |\n| `_promote_nested_generic_methods` | two census SETS as parameters, `_method_frame_key(..., clone.mangled_symbol)`, `mc.coro_frame_key`, `funcs_by_name.get(callee_frame_key(fc))` | `method_might_suspend`/`method_owns_suspension`, `method_key_of_decl`, `method_frame_key_stamped`/`stamp_method_frame_key`, `free_body_of_call` |\n| `_scan_method_callees` | `_suspending_method_target` + `methods_by_key` | `method_target` + `method_id_for_key` |\n| `_structurally_suspends` | `funcs_by_name` then `imported_free_fns`, `nodes[...].edges` | `free_or_imported_body`, `graph_edge_targets` |\n| `_rewrite_drive_sites` | `_method_frame_key(..., inner.resolved_symbol)` / `callee_frame_key(inner)` | `method_key_of_call` / `key_of` |\n| `transform_program` dual-role scan | `callee_frame_key(_fc)` | `key_of` |\n| `transform_program` `nested_method_fbs` + method roots | `_method_frame_key(...)`, `_find_method` | `method_key_of_decl`, `find_entry_method` |\n| `transform_program` splice-drop filter | `callee_frame_key(f)` over `program.functions` | `key_of` |\n| the consumption sweep (3 functions, 4 sites) | `callee_frame_key` | `key_of` |\n\nDELETED, not merely bypassed: `_FrameBuilder._suspends` (and `prepare()`'s\nargument — the set it took was a copy of `closure`), `_MethodTarget`,\n`_suspending_method_target`, `_method_frame_key`, `_node_display`, `_find_method`\n(the last four moved onto the ledger), and\n`typechecker._suspending_methods_set` / `_own_suspending_methods_set` — gone from\n`typechecker/core.py`, since the ledger owns both censuses and nothing outside\nthe transform ever read them. `MethodTarget.suspends` is now `is_suspension`: an\neffect node's `suspends` is a banned raw read and the ledger's own three-valued\nanswer is not, and two different questions should not share a word at the site\nthat asks.\n\nONE BOUNDARY NAMED RATHER THAN CROSSED, and it is the brief's wording I am\nreading narrowly on purpose: the SITE table is a RECORD in U1, not a lookup key.\nFrame builders rewrite the bodies discovery walked — the hoists, the ANF pass and\nbinding uniquification all mint fresh nodes — so a site row cannot be found again\nby node identity once lowering starts, and keying it by (file, line, column)\nwould make a refusal depend on a position the transform itself moved. Consumers\ntherefore read the CALLEE table (today's behavioural path) and the site table is\nthe dump, the decline worklist and U2's input, where node identity still holds\nbecause U2's shape table consults it BEFORE lowering.\n\n## 2. Validation (a) — LEDGER DIFF: zero differences\n\n`--emit-frame-ledger` dumps both tables, sorted (frames by key, sites by every\ncolumn), path-free and node-id-free, because it is diffed between two checkouts.\nTaken at the one boundary that already existed in `transform_program`: after the\nworklist stabilizes and the cycle check runs, BEFORE\n`_instrument_loop_backedges` rewrites a body, so it reports the program the\nauthor wrote.\n\nCorpus: **2597 programs** — every `.saw` under `examples/` (COMPILE-FLAGS\nhonoured, `{TESTDIR}` expanded; `modules/` halves excluded as dependency\nfixtures), `blade/src/main.saw` with its three module paths,\n`libs/{toml,semver,imgformat}`, `selfhost/lexer`, `devtools/{irdet,bench}`, and\nthe READ-ONLY `/Users/shawn/Projects/sawtracker/src/main.saw`. 1670 compiled,\n927 refused (the `errors/` corpus, whose refusal message is recorded so a\nprogram that stops compares as \"refused with this message\" rather than vanishing\nfrom one side).\n\nRun at commit `8b3aaaee` (the instrument over today's predicates, behaviour\nuntouched) and at `75e11046` (the ledger):\n\n    diff -rq ledger_pre ledger_post   ->   0 lines\n\n**Zero differences to classify.** 461 of the dumps carry a frame table at all;\nthe rest are programs with no coroutine root, which report an empty ledger rather\nthan nothing.\n\n## 3. Validation (b) — IR BYTE-COMPARE: zero mismatches\n\n`test_runner.py` on a read-only worktree of the pinned base (`8d752a78`) and on\nthe branch; the two design-220 manifests compared entry by entry.\n\n    base entries: 1579   branch entries: 1579   compared: 1579\n    only in base: 0      only in branch: 0      artifact missing: 0\n    BYTE MISMATCHES: 0\n\nONE normalization, and it is the only thing two worktrees of one commit cannot\nagree on: codegen writes each source's absolute directory into `!DIFile` debug\nmetadata, so the checkout root is substituted on BOTH sides by the same token —\nwhich means a file naming the wrong root would still be caught. Nothing else\ndiffered. (K116 is not in either manifest: design 220 excludes a test carrying\n`COMPILE-FLAGS`.) The base worktree has been removed; it is one\n`git worktree add --detach 8d752a78` to recreate.\n\n## 4. Validation (c) — outcomes\n\n| row set | base | branch |\n|---|---|---|\n| full suite | 2566 passed, 12 xfailed | **2567 passed, 12 xfailed** (+1 = K116; zero XPASS, zero uncited) |\n| `tools/freestanding_runner.py` | — | **36 passed** across riscv32 + arm64 |\n| SL-280: `coro_module_tagged_free_callee_discovery`, the `coro_imported_private_helper_suspends` pin pair, the E1/E2/S0-S9 matrix (`coro_imported_callee_discovery_matrix`), `coro_imported_private_helper_parks_on_io`, `coro_spliced_helper_keeps_its_own_module` | pass | pass |\n| SL-306: `coro_conservative_*` (incl. `..._generic_instance_in_spawned_task`), `coro_nested_suspend_method`, K105's eleven files | pass | pass |\n| K33-K37 (design 223's five embed shapes) | pass, no XFAIL markers | pass |\n| K101-K104, K111-K115 | pass | pass |\n| `coro_existential_only_body_is_not_framed` (SL-323 / K115) | XFAIL | **XFAIL** — the K115 pin is NOT flipped |\n| `coro_generic_call_in_an_expression_position` (SL-326) | XFAIL | XFAIL |\n| `coro_suspending_call_in_inline_catch` (SL-215), `coro_value_inline_catch_hosts_a_move` (SL-231) | XFAIL | XFAIL |\n\nFiltered run of that set: 23 passed, 2 xfailed. No pin flipped in either\ndirection.\n\n## 5. What the ledger RECORDS and U1 does not change — U2's worklist\n\n75 declined FRAME rows across the corpus, in three kinds, all three normal:\n\n- **49** a generic METHOD template (its instantiations are keyed separately; the\n  call site is refused by `_reject_suspending_method_call` unless one was\n  promoted);\n- **15** a generic FREE template (same reason);\n- **11** a driven method ROOT whose AST no method table holds — the drive-site\n  path finds it separately, through the `gsm` table.\n\nFOUR declined SITE rows, and they are the interesting ones — all in ONE file,\n`examples/vector_closure_suspending_transform.saw`, all `context=closure body`:\n\n    SITE ...:48:42  callee=slow_double$m$  declined(no frame was built for this callee)\n    SITE ...:49:25  callee=slow_show$m$    declined(...)\n    SITE ...:53:38  callee=slow_add$m$     declined(...)\n    SITE ...:57:44  callee=slow_add$m$     declined(...)\n\nThe frame table for that program is ONE row, `FRAME traverse$m$ ... causes={}`.\nMECHANISM, read off the two tables together: the driven root has an EMPTY cause\nset, because its three suspending helpers are called from inside\n`Vector.map`/`each`/`fold` CLOSURE LITERALS — the effect graph raises the\nconservative closure-call cause on the METHOD and records no direct edge on the\ncaller — so the closure walk never reaches them, no frame is built, and their\n`yield_now()` runs outside one. It is a PASSING corpus test whose own header\ncomment names the limitation. SL-316's shape, measured rather than inferred, with\na sub-cell SL-316's description does not have: a suspension written DIRECTLY in a\nclosure body lands on the enclosing body's own effect node and is refused\ncleanly, and a CALL to a suspending helper from a closure body is silent.\nRecorded as a comment on SL-316, with the ready-made acceptance row.\n\nNOT FLIPPED, per the unit's contract: SL-323 / K115's `existential_dispatch`\nmembership in `frame_boundary` (the dump shows exactly which bodies are framed on\nthat cause alone — in the corpus, none: every program whose only cause is a\ndispatch is refused at design 88's erased-parameter fence before a frame is\nnamed, which is the pin); SL-324's `might`-where-a-frame-is-meant read in\n`_default_expr_suspends`, recorded in `might_suspend_free`'s docstring; SL-287 /\nSL-316's declines above.\n\n## 6. Conformance rows (obligation 3, first commit)\n\n- **K116** — \"every frame decision is made ONCE, recorded, and read\". New file\n  `examples/conformance/K116_one_frame_ledger_answers_every_discovery_question.saw`\n  + `modules/sl318ledger/lib.saw`, carrying the SL-274/SL-306 review's\n  adversarial probe set as a standing gate. Six cells: three modules declaring\n  one suspending `worker` (entry / an inline submodule / a dependency) answering\n  +1 / +100 / +1000; a module `s_t` beside the module `s.t` each with a\n  suspending `hop` (K110's `module_tag` collision, asked at the frame key); a\n  suspending generic instantiation reached through a NON-generic helper; a\n  conservative-only closure caller whose callee frame must be ABSENT; an imported\n  module-PRIVATE helper reached BY REFERENCE (SL-280's wedge shape); and an\n  imported suspending generic whose mono clone is spliced into the entry module\n  and stays keyed to its HOME module. Every cell asserts the run output AND the\n  IR fact — `__saw_drive_steps` separates a surviving suspension (1) from a\n  dropped one (0), and nine `EXPECT-IR-CONTAINS` / one `EXPECT-IR-ABSENT` name\n  the frames, including `__Frame_hop$m$s_0t` beside `__Frame_hop$m$s_t` and\n  `__Frame_warp$m$sl318ledger$1$Int`. That is the `--emit-ir __Frame_` grep the\n  lead did by hand in three consecutive reviews, pinned.\n- **K117** — \"a ledger MISS is an INVARIANT FAILURE naming the key, never\n  permission to emit a plain call\", covered by the SL-280 pins in `examples/` +\n  K116 + the lane, and recording the boundary U1 deliberately does not cross.\n\nINDEX header recounted: **538 rows, 283 carry a file here, 255 covered\nelsewhere** (was 536/282/254). The pre-existing duplicate K53 row untouched.\n\n## 7. The `corodiscovery` lane\n\n`tools/test_coro_discovery.py`, modelled on `test_producer_taxonomy.py` /\n`test_ast_graft.py`. It PARSES `coro_transform.py` with Python's `ast` — a grep\nis not a gate — and names the builder region by FUNCTION\n(`_build_frame_ledger`), never by a line range, so moving the code cannot\nsilently widen the exemption. A nested `def` is attributed to its enclosing\ntop-level one, which is what keeps the region a single name. Seven checks:\n\n1. no RAW ATTRIBUTE read outside the builder — `.suspends`, `.mangled_symbol`,\n   `.resolved_symbol`, `.module_free_call`, `.mangled_name`, `.coro_frame_key`,\n   in the attribute form AND in the `getattr(x, '<name>', ...)` / `hasattr` form,\n   which is how most of them were actually spelled;\n2. no RAW ANSWER NAME outside it — `callee_frame_key`, `method_frame_key`,\n   `classify_suspensions`, the five U4 derivations, `_suspend_nodes` and the four\n   suspending-method censuses (20 banned inputs in all);\n3. no FRAME TABLE looked up by a node's written `.name`. This is the one the\n   other two cannot see — `funcs_by_name[fc.name]` names no banned attribute and\n   no banned function, and it is SL-280's mechanism exactly. The check names the\n   frame TABLES rather than the `.name` reads, so `encmap[p.name]` and every\n   other binding-name lookup is none of its business;\n4. every consumer in the ledger's docstring census actually reads the ledger, so\n   one cannot be quietly un-routed while its row stays in the table (19 rows);\n5. the composers have an enumerated caller set WITH COUNTS — the ledger's eight,\n   plus the three PRODUCERS that cannot ask a ledger that does not exist yet (the\n   graph's node key, its edge key, the driven-root and spawn-root recording). A\n   new site anywhere fails, and so does a changed count;\n6. `_build_frame_ledger` calls `ledger.freeze()`, and every writer method of\n   `FrameLedger` calls `self._open(...)`;\n7. `FrameLedger.frame` raises `LedgerMiss` and `sawc.py` catches it.\n\nVERIFIED BY INJECTION, not by reading: a consumer given a `getattr(mc,\n'mangled_symbol')`, a `self._tc._suspend_nodes` read, an `fbs` lookup by `.name`\nand a bare `callee_frame_key` call is caught on all four counts; the injection\nwas reverted and the lane is green. Wired into `STAGES` in `tools/battery.sh` as\na fast lane (1s), and into CLAUDE.md's stage list. `--emit-frame-ledger` is\ndocumented in CLAUDE.md's flag block beside `--emit-frame-layout`.\n\n## 8. Gates\n\n- **Per-commit gate** on the commits that touch `sawc/`: full suite +\n  `tools/freestanding_runner.py`, both arches, under the machine-wide lock.\n- **TERMINAL battery**: `tools/battery.sh` full, **29 stages GREEN in 3539s**\n  (28 + the new `corodiscovery`), under the lock. `suite` **2567 passed / 12\n  xfailed**, `freestanding` **36 passed across riscv32 + arm64**, plus `reemit`,\n  `irdet --all`, `gmgate`, `bootstrap`, `corodiff`, `fuzz`, `citations` (13\n  citations: 12 xfail + 1 ledger, 0 stale), `astgraft`, `docverify`, `minivm`.\n  Log: `.build/scratch/battery.log`.\n\n## 9. Filed\n\n- **SL-329** (new) — sawtracker's `Thread.spawn` test worker calls\n  `Command.output`, so the EPIC'S ACCEPTANCE BUILD now stops there, BEFORE\n  SL-215. Found by the corpus sweep, present on main at `8d752a78`,\n  byte-identical in the pre- and post-U1 dumps: U4's newly-correct\n  design-242-ruling-9 refusal (the K112 cell), not U1's. The brief's §0/§2\n  sentence about SL-215 being where the build stops is now stale, and U3's\n  \"sawtracker compiles\" milestone has two gates in front of it. The fix is\n  sawtracker-side (`TaskGroup(threads: 1)`).\n- **SL-316** — commented with the measured corpus cell above.\n\nNothing closed in the tracker. `designs/todo.md`'s design-275 entry marked in\nplace (U1 BUILT + the two measurements + the decline worklist), and U4's bullet\nre-lead as **U4 LANDED Sep 19 (SL-318.p3, merge 668fb0c7)** with the rest\nverbatim. Out of scope and untouched: the state machine, the seven split\nroutines, the drive loop, the executor, design 44's by-value embedding.\n\nBranch diffstat, 12 files: `sawc/coro_ledger.py` +931, `sawc/coro_transform.py`\n(1321 lines changed, net smaller), `tools/test_coro_discovery.py` +532, the K116\nexample +197 and its fixture +46, `sawc/sawc.py` +59, `CLAUDE.md`,\n`designs/todo.md`, `examples/conformance/INDEX.md`,\n`sawc/typechecker/{core,effects}.py`, `tools/battery.sh`.\n","created":"1789844417","id":"c27"},{"author":"agent:claude-sawlang","body":"Submitted patch SL-318.p4 (revision 1): design 275 U1: ONE discovery ledger (sawc/coro_ledger.py) — 23 consumers routed, --emit-frame-ledger, corodiscovery battery lane, K116/K117; behaviour-preserving (ledger dump byte-identical over 2597 programs, optimized IR identical on 1579 rows)","created":"1789845131","id":"c28"},{"author":"agent:claude-sawlang","body":"LEAD VALIDATION of U1 (branch `sl318u1` tip 75e11046, 4 commits over main 8d752a78) — SUBMITTED as SL-318.p4 (Sep 19).\n\nReran on the branch, under the suite lock: the `coro_`/`conformance/`/`sync_`/`thread_`/`spawn` filter — 590 passed, 5 xfailed (SL-267, SL-323, SL-326, SL-215, SL-231 — the expected set, zero XPASS). `tools/test_coro_discovery.py` passes (20 raw inputs banned outside `_build_frame_ledger`, 19 consumers on the ledger, 2 composers with one calling module, freeze + miss invariants present). Read the ledger's API + census docstring, the miss path (`LedgerMiss` → `_report_ice`), the lane's seven checks, the CLI/CLAUDE.md/INDEX/todo hunks and the `effects.py` consumer-docstring updates. Branch is clean and rebased on 8d752a78 (merge-base = main).\n\nDETERMINISM of the instrument: `--emit-frame-ledger` on K116 under `PYTHONHASHSEED=1` and `=9` is byte-identical (22 FRAME rows, 19 SITE rows; the rows read correctly — the three same-name `worker` frames keyed `$m$`, `$m$inner`, `$m$sl318ledger`; `s_0t` vs `s_t`; the template row `buildable=no(a generic template owns no frame …)` beside its `mono-instance` row; `closure_root` with `causes={}` `boundary=no`).\n\nADVERSARIAL PROBES beyond K116 (two-module same-name STRUCT + suspending METHOD, a worklist-depth chain public generic → private generic → private suspending leaf, spawned-in-driven): compiled on BOTH compilers, every result byte-identical between main and the branch — U1 changed no behaviour at any cell. Two of the cells are PRE-EXISTING refusals of sound programs, filed as U2 worklist input (neither is a U1 regression; both fail before discovery finishes, so neither reaches the ledger):\n- **SL-330** — a module-QUALIFIED call to an imported suspending GENERIC free function inside a driven body is never framed (`alpha.outer<Int>(n)`: `__Frame_chain.resume` plain-calls `outer$1$Int`, refused by the sync check). Bare import works, qualified non-generic works (K116): the cell is qualified × generic × embedded — SL-217's embedded sibling; mechanism named (the promotion walks `FunctionCall`s, a qualified call is a `MethodCall` with `module_free_call`).\n- **SL-331** — an ENTRY-module template instantiated from inside another template cannot name a suspending free function (`undefined function leaf` at the nested clone); the sync twin, the direct-`yield_now` twin and the IMPORTED twin all work. Mechanism hypothesis: the nested clone is typechecked in the pristine-template capture's namespace, right for an imported template and wrong for an entry-module one.\n\nPer the unit's contract nothing flipped: SL-323/K115 stays XFAIL (the ledger's `boundary` column now shows which bodies are framed on `existential_dispatch` alone — a ruling for the user), SL-324 recorded in `might_suspend_free`'s docstring, the four `declined` SITE rows (SL-316's shape, `vector_closure_suspending_transform.saw`) recorded not refused. **SL-329** (the sawtracker acceptance build now stops at U4's correct `Command.output`-in-`Thread.spawn` refusal, BEFORE SL-215) is a sawtracker-side change and a brief §0/§2 sentence to update — raised to the user for re-sequencing.\n\nGate is the server's; review per the Sep-11 delegation. On approval + green: merge, ff, U1-landed note here, remove `sl318u1`, then dispatch U2 (totality) with SL-330/SL-331/SL-316's four rows/SL-326 as its worklist seeds.\n","created":"1789845356","id":"c29"},{"author":"agent:sawtracker-tests","body":"Tests passed for SL-318.p4 (revision 1)","created":"1789845612","id":"c30"},{"author":"agent:codex","body":"Requested changes on patch SL-318.p4 (revision 1)\n\nReviewed SL-318.p4 revision 1 (base 84986108, applied d97f0be2). Request changes: two gaps in the agreed U1 ledger contract remain despite the passing behavior-preservation checks.\n\n[P1] Enforce the missing-decision invariant on the consumer path, not an unused lookup.\nsawc/coro_ledger.py:626-629; sawc/coro_transform.py:11910-11917.\nNormal compilation never populates _frames or _sites: _record_frame_ledger_rows is conditional on capture_enabled(). More importantly, no compiler consumer calls FrameLedger.frame(), the only read that raises LedgerMiss. free_call_frame() still translates membership failure in _built_free directly to None, and the call classifiers use that as the ordinary-call answer. method_target() likewise re-derives its answer from call annotations/censuses instead of reading a recorded decision. Thus an undiscovered callee remains indistinguishable from an explicitly discovered non-suspending or declined callee. The new gate passes because it checks that frame() contains a raise, not that actual consumer misses reach it.\n\nConcrete probe: create FrameLedger(SimpleNamespace(), {}, None), freeze it, then call free_call_frame(SimpleNamespace(name=\"missing$m$entry\")). It returns None; frame(\"missing$m$entry\") raises LedgerMiss, but that strict method is unused. tools/test_coro_discovery.py still passes. Populate the authoritative decisions independently of dump capture and route lowering through checked reads; keep explicit negative/declined outcomes so existing behavior stays unchanged. Add a regression that injects an unknown decision into a real consumer path and requires the named-key ICE. This is the U1 miss invariant, not a request to perform U2's semantic flip of already-recorded declines.\n\n[P2] Include driven method root bodies in the SITE census.\nsawc/coro_transform.py:11216-11222.\nThe site walk visits ledger.closure and ledger.method_closure, but driven method roots are deliberately separate from method_closure. Their FRAME rows can therefore exist while every suspension in their bodies is omitted from the dump. Reproduction:\n  .venv/bin/python sawc/sawc.py examples/coro_driving_method_self.saw --emit-frame-ledger\nThe patch emits FRAME Counter_climb with causes={test_suspend}, boundary=yes, buildable=yes, followed by \"# sites: 0\", although Counter.climb contains __saw_suspend(). That site should be recorded with context=driven root and outcome=inline. Include all driven method-root bodies (deduplicating roots also embedded), and pin their site rows, including generic root variants. Otherwise the advertised per-position instrument and the U2 worklist omit a supported root family; comparing identical omissions in old/new dumps cannot validate coverage.\n\nValidation: independently ran corodiscovery (passes), the focused K116 adversarial matrix and coro_driving_method_self runtime tests (2/2 pass), and the ledger-dump / missing-key probes above. The server reports the full compiler suite and both freestanding targets passing for d97f0be2. Those results support preserved behavior, but do not establish the new closed-ledger and complete-site guarantees.","created":"1789863919","id":"c31"},{"author":"agent:claude-sawlang","body":"LEAD ACK of codex's SL-318.p4 r1 review (changes-requested, two findings) — both accepted; the U1 agent is RESUMED in `sl318u1` to fix them, and the lead revises to r2 on its report.\n\n[P1] accepted as the sharper reading of U1's own contract: \"a MISS is an invariant failure\" is only true if consumers take CHECKED reads and the decision tables are populated on every compile, not only under `--emit-frame-ledger` capture. The fix routes the lowering consumers through checked reads with explicit negative/declined rows carrying today's outcomes (behaviour unchanged; only a genuine miss ICEs), populates the tables unconditionally (capture decides rendering only), and adds an injection regression on a real consumer path. The class question — which reads legitimately answer by absence (a call that is not a nameable callee) versus which are misses — is answered per read, in the docstrings.\n\n[P2] accepted: driven method roots are a supported root family and their sites belong in the census (`context=driven root`, `outcome=inline` for `coro_driving_method_self.saw`'s `__saw_suspend()`), with pins incl. the generic-root variants; the corpus-wide dump diff is regenerated at both ends afterwards.\n\nValidation note for r2: the lead's adversarial probes (SL-330/SL-331, c29) remain pre-existing and are unaffected by either fix.\n","created":"1789864005","id":"c32"},{"author":"agent:claude-sl318u1","body":"# U1 ADDENDUM — codex r1's two findings fixed (branch tip `b99eba7e`, 5 commits)\n\nBoth accepted as stated. `b99eba7e` is the fifth commit on `sl318u1`\n(`207c7e0f` -> `8b3aaaee` -> `b277742b` -> `75e11046` -> `b99eba7e`), still on\nmain `8d752a78`. Not submitted.\n\n## P1 — the miss invariant is on the CONSUMER path now\n\nCodex was right on all three counts: `_record_frame_decisions` ran only under\n`capture_enabled()`, so an ordinary build had NO decision table; `free_call_frame`\nturned a `_built_free` membership failure straight into `None`, which the five\nclassifiers read as the ordinary-call answer; and the one read that raised —\n`frame()` — had no caller. The gate passed because it checked that `frame()`\ncontained a raise, not that a miss reached it.\n\nThree changes make the invariant real, and none of them changes what the\ntransform does:\n\n1. **The decision is THREE-VALUED and recorded on EVERY compile.** `FrameRow`\n   gains `decision` ∈ {`framed`, `no-frame-owed`, `declined`}; the flag decides\n   only whether the dump TEXT is rendered. A decided negative and a gap are\n   different facts now, which is the whole of P1.\n2. **The SITE census CLOSES the frame table as it walks.** A callee it meets with\n   no decision yet gets one: `no-frame-owed` when it owns no suspension, and\n   `declined(the discovery walk did not reach this callee)` when it does — that\n   third case being SL-287/SL-316's shape, which U1 records and lowers exactly as\n   before. So every key a lowering consumer can ask about has a row.\n3. **`free_call_frame` and `method_target`'s EMBED answer are CHECKED READS.**\n   A recorded decision answers; no row for a callee that owns a suspension raises\n   `LedgerMiss` naming the key, and `sawc.py` reports it through `_report_ice`.\n   `_owns_a_suspension_unrecorded` asks EXACTLY the two questions the walk asked —\n   the design-275-U4 `frame_boundary` derivation, and design 96's STRUCTURAL\n   route, whose cache `freeze(structural=…)` now carries over — so it is an\n   invariant rather than a heuristic. Generic TEMPLATES are excluded: they own no\n   frame by construction, and a call still naming one is `_classify_call`'s clean\n   design-70 refusal, not an ICE.\n\n### The checked-read matrix\n\nEvery read in the census table got a verdict, because a read that answers \"no\" by\nabsence cannot tell a decided negative from a gap. Each verdict is written into\nits own docstring and tabled in `FrameLedger`'s:\n\n| read | absence is... | why |\n|---|---|---|\n| `free_call_frame` | **A MISS** | The read the five call-site classifiers ask. A recorded `framed` / `no-frame-owed` / `declined` answers; no row for a callee that OWNS a suspension is `LedgerMiss` naming the key. Absence is the answer only for what is not a frame question at all: a genuine METHOD call (`callee_frame_key` answers None), an `extern`, a builtin, a call the transform itself synthesized, a std free function with no body in this unit, and a generic TEMPLATE |\n| `method_target` (EMBED only) | **A MISS**, once frozen | An EMBED names a frame key, and the census records a decision for every method call in every body that will be lowered. NOT SUSPENDING names no frame and UNSUPPORTED is a refusal whose point is that none could be named — neither owes a row |\n| `frame` | **A MISS** | The explicit strict lookup, unchanged |\n| `is_built` | legitimate | `_analyze_nesting` asks it of every node on a suspending-call CYCLE, and the graph is WIDER than the frames. A node the walk declined is exactly what it must answer `no` for — SL-227's gate, without which blade's own resolver is refused |\n| `might_suspend_free` | legitimate | A GRAPH read. No effect node means no suspension source, which is what `no` means; a defaulted argument naming an `extern` has no node and needs none |\n| `instantiation_is_boundary` | legitimate | A graph read, asked BEFORE the freeze. An instance the registry never built has no node, and `no` sends the call to codegen's ordinary monomorphization |\n| `free_body` / `free_or_imported_body` / `importable_body` | legitimate | \"This unit holds no such free-function body\" is a fact, not a gap |\n| `method_id_for_key` / `method_entry_for_key` | legitimate at the table | …and the census turns it into a recorded `declined(no registered method AST answers this frame key)` where it matters |\n\n### The injection regression, and how it fires\n\n`tools/test_coro_discovery.py` check 8, and it is a REAL COMPILE rather than a\nstructural claim — which is the point codex made. It injects a one-key skip into\n`FrameLedger.record_frame`, restores the file in a `finally` (the\n`test_ice_breadcrumb.py` pattern this repo already uses for exactly this), and\nruns sawc twice on `examples/coro_nested_suspend_two_deep.saw` (`outer -> mid ->\ninner`, all three driven, `inner` suspending):\n\n* `SAW_LEDGER_DROP_KEY=inner$m$` — `mid`'s body calls a suspending callee with no\n  row, which is the consumer path, and the compile must fail with\n\n      error: internal compiler error: the coroutine discovery ledger has no\n      decision for the suspending callee `inner$m$`, which a body being lowered\n      calls. Every callee that owns a suspension is decided about before any body\n      is lowered — framed, or declined with a reason — so an absent row is a\n      discovery gap, never `does not suspend` (design 275 U1)\n\n  ONE line, NAMING the key, no traceback. The check asserts all three.\n* nothing dropped — the same compile must SUCCEED, so the injection is the only\n  difference and the regression cannot pass vacuously.\n\nCheck 7 grew two teeth beside it: BOTH strict reads (`frame` and\n`free_call_frame`) must raise `LedgerMiss`, and the lane FAILS if\n`_record_frame_decisions` is ever put back under a `capture_enabled()` guard.\nVerified by injection in the other direction too: re-adding that guard makes the\nlane report the guard AND makes an ordinary build of\n`coro_nested_suspend_two_deep.saw` ICE — which is the clearest evidence that the\nchecked read is genuinely on the lowering path.\n\nAn honesty note on codex's synthetic probe: `FrameLedger(SimpleNamespace(), {},\nNone)` still answers `None`, and that is correct — an EMPTY ledger has no body\ntable, no graph and no cause set, so there is nothing to ask. The invariant is\nover a ledger discovery actually built, which is why the regression drops a row\nfrom a real compile. That reasoning is in `_owns_a_suspension_unrecorded`'s\ndocstring.\n\n## P2 — driven method roots are in the site census\n\nConfirmed the repro exactly: `FRAME Counter_climb … boundary=yes` beside\n`# sites: 0`. Discovery now resolves every driven method ROOT's declaration AFTER\nthe walk, from the same two sources the lowering loop uses (the typechecker's\nper-instantiation clone for a method on a GENERIC STRUCT — which lives in no\nextension, so no method table holds it — and `find_entry_method` for a plain\none), into `ledger.method_root_decls`. Deliberately a table of its OWN and\ndeliberately after the walk: putting a root into `methods_by_id` would change\nwhich method edges the walk follows, and the walk is over. The census then walks\nthose bodies with `context=driven root`, deduped by body identity against the\nembedded scan so a method that is both a root and an embedded callee is reported\nonce, as the root it is.\n\n    $ sawc examples/coro_driving_method_self.saw --emit-frame-ledger\n    FRAME Counter_climb  kind=method  causes={test_suspend}  boundary=yes  decision=framed  buildable=yes  home=coro_driving_method_self  splice=entry\n    # sites: 1\n    SITE coro_driving_method_self.saw:16:9  callee=__saw_suspend  context=driven root  outcome=inline\n\nCheck 9 pins one program per root family and requires the FRAME row AND at least\none `context=driven root` SITE row in each: `coro_driving_method_self.saw` (a\nplain driven method), `coro_generic_struct_method.saw` (a driven method on a\nGENERIC STRUCT), `coro_generic_method_self.saw` (a METHOD-LEVEL generic). It\nasserts presence rather than exact line numbers, so editing an example cannot\nmake it fail spuriously while deleting the census still does.\n\n### Site counts across the corpus, before and after\n\nSite rows total **2035 -> 2044**: NINE new rows, ALL `context=driven root`, zero\nnew rows of any other context and zero rows lost. Per file, and SIX of the nine\nfiles went from `# sites: 0`:\n\n| file | sites before | after |\n|---|---|---|\n| `coro_driving_method_self.saw` | 0 | 1 |\n| `coro_generic_method_self.saw` | 0 | 1 |\n| `coro_ref_self_method.saw` | 0 | 1 |\n| `coro_generic_mono_type_subst.saw` | 0 | 2 |\n| `coro_generic_struct_method.saw` | 0 | 2 |\n| `coro_generic_struct_and_method.saw` | 0 | 4 |\n| `conformance/V95_materialized_capture_releases_once.saw` | 1 | 2 |\n| `conformance/K22_cross_module_generic_embed_private_sibling.saw` | 2 | 3 |\n| `coro_cross_module_generic.saw` | 4 | 6 |\n\nEvery one of those nine is a driven method root whose body the census previously\nskipped — which is why comparing identical omissions in the r1 dumps could not\nhave caught it, exactly as codex said.\n\n## Validation (a), re-run and CLASSIFIED — residue ZERO\n\nThe dump changed on purpose (P1's `decision` column, P1's closure of the table,\nP2's site rows), so the r1 byte-identical comparison is no longer the right\ninstrument. Re-ran the corpus dump at this tip over the same 2597 programs and\nclassified every line of the delta against the pre-U1 instrument's dumps:\n\n    files compared: 2597\n    FRAME rows differing only by the new `decision=` column:            1594\n    NEW frame rows, decision=no-frame-owed:                              930\n    NEW frame rows, decision=declined (unreached / no method AST):         3\n    frame rows CORRECTED by P2 (bogus root declines -> real framed rows):  11\n    frame rows whose `splice` origin is now reported truthfully:           5\n    NEW site rows with context=driven root:                                9\n    NEW site rows that are NOT driven roots:                               0\n    site rows total: old 2035 -> new 2044\n    RESIDUE (unexplained differences):                                     0\n\nNothing vanished on either table. The 11 corrections are P2's other half: a root\nthe old census could not resolve was filed through the FREE-row path, so it\nreported `kind=free causes={} home=-` — it is a real `mono-instance`/`method`\nrow with its true cause set and home now. The 5 splice corrections are the census\nclosing the table over the IMPORTED body table too, so a body that lives only\nthere reports `splice=imported` instead of defaulting to `entry`.\n\n## Validation (b), re-run — the format-independent behaviour proof\n\n`test_runner.py` on a fresh read-only worktree of the pinned base `8d752a78` and\non this tip, design-220 manifests compared artifact by artifact:\n\n    base entries: 1579   branch entries: 1579   compared: 1579\n    only in base: 0      only in branch: 0      artifact missing: 0\n    BYTE MISMATCHES: 0\n\n(one normalization, the checkout root in `!DIFile` debug metadata, substituted on\nboth sides by the same token). The base worktree has been removed again.\n\n## U2's worklist, updated\n\n67 declined FRAME rows (was 75): 49 generic METHOD templates and 15 generic FREE\ntemplates — both normal — plus the THREE the census found the walk never reached:\n`slow_double$m$`, `slow_show$m$`, `slow_add$m$` in\n`examples/vector_closure_suspending_transform.saw`. The 11 bogus root declines\nare gone. FOUR declined SITE rows, unchanged, all in that same file and all\n`context=closure body`. So P1's closure of the table made the SL-316 cell visible\nfrom the FRAME side as well as the SITE side, which is the cleanest statement of\nit: three callees that own a suspension, no frame, `declined(the discovery walk\ndid not reach this callee)`.\n\nNOT flipped, per the unit's contract: SL-323 / K115's `existential_dispatch`\nmembership, SL-324's `might`-where-a-frame-is-meant read, SL-287/SL-316's\ndeclines.\n\n## Gates at the final tip `b99eba7e`\n\n* Per-commit: full suite **2567 passed / 12 xfailed** (zero XPASS, zero uncited)\n  + `tools/freestanding_runner.py` **36 passed across riscv32 + arm64**, under the\n  machine-wide lock.\n* TERMINAL battery: `tools/battery.sh` full, **29 stages GREEN in 3413s**,\n  including `corodiscovery` (now 15s — two of its nine checks run the compiler),\n  `reemit`, `irdet --all`, `gmgate`, `bootstrap`, `corodiff`, `fuzz`, `citations`\n  (13 citations: 12 xfail + 1 ledger, 0 stale), `astgraft`, `docverify`,\n  `minivm`, `freestanding`. Log: `.build/scratch/battery_r2.log`.\n\nDocs updated with the fixes: `FrameLedger`'s docstring carries the checked-read\nmatrix, K117's INDEX row records why the r1 invariant was decorative and what\nreplaced it, CLAUDE.md's `corodiscovery` entry names the two compiler-running\nchecks, and `designs/todo.md`'s U1 bullet carries this round. Nothing closed in\nthe tracker; no new findings from this round.\n","created":"1789870089","id":"c33"}],"created":"1789738775","description":"USER RULING (Sep 18): revisit the coroutine transform's discovery seams as an EPIC rather than continue per-site funnel fixes. Four units, all ruled as the lead recommended: (1) ONE DISCOVERY LEDGER — a single pass computes the closed frame table (key, declaration, really-suspends, buildable, reason) before any body is lowered; classifier, closure walk, generic promotion, rejectors and drive-site rewriting READ it and derive nothing; a battery lane fails on any transform site that reads the raw suspension bit or a symbol name directly (the producer-taxonomy / astgraft precedent). (2) TOTALITY — every statement and expression shape is either splittable or refused by ONE rejector reading ONE shape table; a callee the walk cannot frame is a compile error, never a plain call whose park blocks the executor (closes SL-287, SL-316; the K33-K37 XFAIL family are rows). (3) COVERAGE UNITS INSIDE THE EPIC — SL-215 (inline try/catch split) and SL-317 (iterator state in the frame for a collection for) are epic units, NOT standalone point fixes; sawtracker's build (currently stopped at those two) is the epic's acceptance test and WAITS for it — the user ruled a point fix is a waste of time. (4) A THREE-VALUED ANSWER AT THE EFFECT SOURCE — the graph answers once (really suspends / only through a closure call / does not) instead of one bit meaning might plus design 206's second question bolted beside it. Census (Sep 18): coro_transform.py 12,224 lines, a 212-method _FrameBuilder, a 1,032-line transform_program with nine nested closures, ~20 distinct suspension/frame-key predicates, 7 split shapes (if, if-let, guard-let, while, RANGE for, match, BLOCK try-catch), 21 scattered refusals, 17 governing designs (44..224). The lowering is stable; every finding this week (SL-280, SL-274 keying sites, SL-306 + its review, SL-215, SL-317, SL-287, SL-316) was a discovery seam. Brief: designs/275-coro-discovery-uniformity.md (lead, next). Sequenced after SL-306.p1 r2 + SL-274.p1 r2 land and the small-fixes batch; ledger unit first.","id":"SL-318","labels":["epic","coro","design-275"],"priority":"high","project":"SL","revision":36,"sequence":1051,"stage":"queued","status":"open","title":"EPIC: coroutine transform discovery uniformity — one frame ledger, total shape coverage, no silent decline (design 275)","updated":"1789870089"},"id":"SL-318","project":"SL","request":"{\"action\":\"patch_revise\",\"actor\":\"agent:claude-sawlang\",\"diff\":\"diff --git a/CLAUDE.md b/CLAUDE.md\\nindex 3c827da2..ec213618 100644\\n--- a/CLAUDE.md\\n+++ b/CLAUDE.md\\n@@ -61,14 +61,21 @@ activated first.\\n ./.venv/bin/python sawc/sawc.py <src.saw> [-o out] [-v] [-c]\\n     [--emit-ir] [--emit-ast] [--ids] [--emit-docs] [--emit-docs-all]\\n     [-O0 | -O2 | -Os | -Oz]\\n-    [--emit-frame-layout] [--emit-bt-table]\\n+    [--emit-frame-layout] [--emit-bt-table] [--emit-frame-ledger]\\n     [--target TRIPLE] [--target-features FEATURES]\\n     [--module-path NAME=DIR]\\n     [--freestanding] [--runtime-build] [--runtime-provider]\\n     [--no-hidden-alloc] [-W NAME | -W all]\\n ```\\n That is the complete flag set (`sawc.py:2081-2190`); `-o` defaults to\\n-`.build/<source>`. `--no-hidden-alloc` (design 135) rejects the\\n+`.build/<source>`. `--emit-frame-ledger` (design 275 U1) dumps the\\n+coroutine transform's DISCOVERY LEDGER instead of code — one FRAME row\\n+per frame key it reached (kind, suspension causes, buildable + why not,\\n+home module, where the body came from) and one SITE row per suspension\\n+position (its context and outcome: embed / inline / refuse / declined).\\n+Deterministically ordered and path-free, so two compilers' dumps diff:\\n+it is the instrument behaviour preservation is proven with, and the\\n+`declined` rows are U2's worklist. `--no-hidden-alloc` (design 135) rejects the\\n allocations the compiler inserts that no source construct names.\\n `-W` (design 150) enables a warning category (repeatable, `-W all` for\\n every one; warnings are off by default and never affect the exit code).\\n@@ -172,6 +179,19 @@ objects are built + cached under `.build/rt/` and auto-linked (delete\\n   tools/battery.sh --list\\n   ```\\n   Stages: `suite`, `icebreadcrumb`, `lexdiff`, `astdiff`, `astgraft`,\\n+  `corodiscovery` (design 275 U1: ONE ledger answers every coroutine frame\\n+  decision — `tools/test_coro_discovery.py` parses `coro_transform.py` and\\n+  fails on any site outside the ledger's builder that reads a raw discovery\\n+  input, on a frame table looked up by a written `.name`, on a named consumer\\n+  that stopped reading the ledger, on a new caller of either key composer, and\\n+  on a missing freeze or miss invariant. Two of its nine checks RUN the\\n+  compiler rather than reading it: one DROPS a recorded decision — injected\\n+  into `FrameLedger.record_frame`, restored in a `finally`, the\\n+  `icebreadcrumb` pattern — and requires the internal-compiler-error line to\\n+  NAME the dropped key, because a structural check that `frame()` contains a\\n+  raise is not a check that a consumer's miss reaches it; the other asserts the\\n+  dump carries a `context=driven root` site for each of the three driven-root\\n+  families, which is what caught a FRAME row sitting beside `# sites: 0`),\\n   `citations` (DF-248c, Aug 24: stale XFAIL/ledger citations against the\\n   tracker's closed set + committed conflict markers over tracked files —\\n   the gate for the files nothing compiles),\\ndiff --git a/designs/todo.md b/designs/todo.md\\nindex 7c036847..27eb81b5 100644\\n--- a/designs/todo.md\\n+++ b/designs/todo.md\\n@@ -38,7 +38,8 @@ is scheduled and in what order is the whole of what they say.\\n - Design 259 — the self-hosted parser (designs/259-selfhost-parser.md; QUEUED Sep 1 by the user, §3 ruling batch fully RULED same day incl. R7′ statement arms). **RECONCILED by design 274 (DRAFT Sep 17, designs/274-parser-track-reconciliation.md; tracker SL-2):** the doctrine + rulings stand; U2 was superseded by the Sep-10 arena ruling and landed as the prototype track (SL-300: M18/M19/M20); U0 + U1 (the Python grammar debt + depth funnel, never started) become one compiler dispatch gated on the prototype's debt probes; N10 CLOSED by probe; the N-findings get SL ids at 274's approval\\n \\n - Design 275 — coroutine transform discovery uniformity (designs/275-coro-discovery-uniformity.md; RULED Sep 18 by the user, epic SL-318). Four units in order U4 → U1 → U2 → U3: the effect answer at the source, the frame LEDGER + `corodiscovery` gate lane, TOTALITY (no silent decline; SL-287, SL-316, K33–K37), then coverage SL-215 + SL-317 as epic units — NOT point fixes; sawtracker's build waits for the finished foundation (user ruling). Dispatches right after SL-274.p1 lands, AHEAD of the small-fixes batch (user re-sequenced Sep 18); one Opus agent per unit, serially\\n-  - **U4 BUILT (branch `sl318u4`, awaiting the lead's validation + patch).** The answer is a CAUSE SET per node, not the three values the brief drafted (lead amendment, Sep 18: a three-valued answer would have forced each consumer to re-derive its own subset privately, which is the secondary analysis the unit removes). ONE set-union pass over the effect graph (`effects.classify_suspensions`) computes which causes reach each node — `cooperative`, `blocking`, `test_suspend`, `closure_call`, plus `existential_dispatch`, a FIFTH cause the sweep found nobody had classified — and the four walkers it replaces (`suspends`, design 206's `really_suspending`, design 242's `suspends_ignoring_blocking`, SL-306's `suspends_ignoring_closure_calls`) are DELETED, each becoming one of five named derivations whose docstring lists its consumers: `might_suspend`, `wraps_main`, `refused_in_thread_body`, `frame_boundary`, `closure_only`. The std seed carries the SET — and, since the lead's review, a SOURCE PER CAUSE beside it — which fixed a cell nobody had measured (a `Thread.spawn` body was permitted to call a std method that parks cooperatively, because the seeded leaf answered off one representative label; the refusal then had nothing left to name and printed a `<suspension source>` placeholder). Conformance rows K111-K115 are the cause × context matrix. Findings recorded, not absorbed: SL-323, SL-324, SL-325, SL-326, SL-327\\n+  - **U1 BUILT (branch `sl318u1`, awaiting the lead's validation + patch).** `sawc/coro_ledger.py::FrameLedger` is the ONE authority for every frame decision — key, kind, cause set, buildable-with-reason, home module, splice origin — built by `_build_frame_ledger` (a worklist to stabilization, extracted out of `transform_program`) and FROZEN before any body is lowered; a write after the freeze and a MISS at a consumer both raise `LedgerMiss`, which `sawc.py` reports as an internal compiler error naming the key. Nineteen consumers routed (the census table is the ledger's docstring); `self._suspends` and the two `typechecker._*_suspending_methods_set` censuses are GONE, and `frame_keys.callee_frame_key` + `coro_ledger.method_frame_key` survive as the ledger's private composers — the transform no longer imports either. `--emit-frame-ledger` dumps both tables deterministically, and the `corodiscovery` battery lane (`tools/test_coro_discovery.py`, 9 checks — seven parse the module, two RUN the compiler) fails on any raw discovery read outside the builder. BEHAVIOUR-PRESERVING, measured: the dump is byte-identical across 2597 programs (examples + blade + libs + selfhost + devtools + the read-only sawtracker sources) before and after, and the design-220 optimized-IR artifacts match the pinned base (8d752a78) on all 1579 rows. U2's worklist, read off the dumps: 67 declined FRAME rows (49 generic METHOD templates, 15 generic FREE templates — both normal — and THREE the census found the walk never reached) and FOUR declined SITE rows, all in `examples/vector_closure_suspending_transform.saw` and all `context=closure body`: the effect graph gives `traverse` NO cause at all (its suspending calls sit inside `Vector.map`/`each`/`fold` closure literals, which raise the conservative cause on the METHOD and no direct edge on the caller), so the walk never reaches `slow_double`/`slow_show`/`slow_add`, no frame is built, and their `yield_now()` runs outside one — a PASSING corpus test whose own comment names the limitation. ONE set-union pass over the effect graph (`effects.classify_suspensions`) computes which causes reach each node — `cooperative`, `blocking`, `test_suspend`, `closure_call`, plus `existential_dispatch`, a FIFTH cause the sweep found nobody had classified — and the four walkers it replaces (`suspends`, design 206's `really_suspending`, design 242's `suspends_ignoring_blocking`, SL-306's `suspends_ignoring_closure_calls`) are DELETED, each becoming one of five named derivations whose docstring lists its consumers: `might_suspend`, `wraps_main`, `refused_in_thread_body`, `frame_boundary`, `closure_only`. Both readings are correct and they are different questions — which is the whole argument for a cause SET over a single bit, since a body carrying this cause AND a cooperative one is refused and framed alike. It is conservative — a vtable word carries no effect, and design 223 refuses the dispatch outright when some conformance body really suspends — so no frame is ever owed for it, and design 206's predicate struck it. SL-306's did not, and the transform frames on SL-306's: a body whose only cause is a dispatch joins the driven closure, and design 88's fence then refuses its erased `&any Trait` PARAMETER for a frame it never needed, on a program with no suspension in it. U4 gives the cause its own bit and keeps it inside `frame_boundary`, because the unit is behaviour-preserving by construction; the membership is recorded in that derivation's docstring rather than settled, and the pin's XPASS is what will validate the ruling when SL-318's ledger unit makes it |\\n+| K116 | every frame decision is made ONCE, recorded, and read: one ledger answers \\\"does this callee suspend / what key names its frame / can it be built\\\", and no consumer re-derives it from the raw inputs | `K116_one_frame_ledger_answers_every_discovery_question.saw` + `coro_imported_callee_discovery_matrix.saw` + `coro_module_tagged_free_callee_discovery.saw` + `coro_spliced_helper_keeps_its_own_module.saw` + `flat_and_nested_module_paths_are_distinct.saw` + `coro_conservative_free_callee_not_framed.saw` | design 275 U1 (SL-318). K101-K105 are five faces of ONE mechanism, and this row is the mechanism itself: a DECISION re-derived at every consumer, policed by reading. Twenty-odd predicates in `coro_transform.py` each asked the raw inputs — `node.suspends`, `mangled_symbol`, `resolved_symbol`, `module_free_call`, a callee node's written `name`, `_suspending_methods_set` / `_own_suspending_methods_set`, the entry and imported body tables, the instantiation registry, the method tables — and every finding of the week of Sep 14 was two of those readings disagreeing: SL-280's six keying sites, SL-274's review finding two more the funnel had not reached, SL-306's classifier-versus-closure-walk split, and its own review finding a fourth reader of the broad bit. `sawc/coro_ledger.py::FrameLedger` is the one authority now: a worklist runs to stabilization BEFORE any body is lowered, the ledger FREEZES, and `callee_frame_key` / `method_frame_key` survive only as its private composers — so a new consumer cannot ask a raw input even by accident, which is what the `corodiscovery` battery lane (`tools/test_coro_discovery.py`) enforces by parsing the module. THE FILE IS THE ADVERSARIAL SET from the SL-274 and SL-306 reviews, promoted from lead probes to a standing gate: three modules declaring one suspending `worker` (entry, an inline submodule, a dependency) answering +1 / +100 / +1000; a module `s_t` beside the module `s.t` (K110's `module_tag` collision, at the frame key); a suspending generic instantiation reached through a NON-generic helper (the depth the seed walk alone does not reach); a conservative-only closure caller, whose frames must be ABSENT; an imported module-PRIVATE helper reached by REFERENCE (the SL-280 wedge's own shape); and an imported generic whose mono clone is spliced into the entry module and stays keyed to its HOME module. Every cell asserts the RUN OUTPUT and the framed-versus-unframed IR fact, because each program prints the same number either way — `__saw_drive_steps` separates a surviving suspension (1) from a dropped one (0) and the `__Frame_` grep names the frames, which is the check the lead did by hand in three consecutive reviews |\\n+| K117 | a ledger MISS at a consumer is an INVARIANT FAILURE naming the key — never permission to emit a plain call | `coro_imported_private_helper_suspends.saw` + `coro_imported_private_helper_parks_on_io.saw` + `K116_one_frame_ledger_answers_every_discovery_question.saw` + `errors/coro_suspending_recursion.saw` | design 275 U1, and the half that decides what a gap COSTS rather than whether one exists. Before the ledger, a consumer whose key matched nothing simply answered \\\"does not suspend\\\" — the same answer a genuinely sync callee gets — so a discovery gap and a correct negative were indistinguishable, and K102's face of it was a park lowered through codegen's out-of-frame fallback, which registers the fd and then parks the cooperative executor's OWN thread: a whole process stopped on one idle socket, exit code 0, nothing said. The ledger's read API raises an ICE breadcrumb naming the key instead, so the failure is a located compiler bug rather than a wedged program — at `free_call_frame`, which is the read the five call-site classifiers ask, not at a strict lookup nothing calls (codex's SL-318.p4 r1 review found the first draft's invariant decorative for exactly that reason: the decision tables were populated only under `--emit-frame-ledger`, and absence still carried the negative on the lowering path). The DECISION is three-valued now — `framed` / `no-frame-owed` / `declined(reason)` — so a decided negative and a gap are different facts, and `tools/test_coro_discovery.py` proves the gap reaches the consumer by DROPPING one recorded decision and requiring the compiler to name the dropped key. NOTE THE BOUNDARY U1 DELIBERATELY DOES NOT CROSS: a site the transform DECLINES today — a callee or closure body the walk skipped and lowered as a plain call (SL-287, SL-316) — is RECORDED in the ledger's site table with outcome `declined(reason)` and lowered exactly as before, because U1 is behaviour-preserving by construction. Turning those declines into refusals is U2's flip and owes U2's consumer sweep; `--emit-frame-ledger` dumps the list, which is what makes the worklist auditable instead of remembered |\\n \\n ## Visibility and module boundaries\\n \\ndiff --git a/examples/conformance/K116_one_frame_ledger_answers_every_discovery_question.saw b/examples/conformance/K116_one_frame_ledger_answers_every_discovery_question.saw\\nnew file mode 100644\\nindex 00000000..59ea63ed\\n--- /dev/null\\n+++ b/examples/conformance/K116_one_frame_ledger_answers_every_discovery_question.saw\\n@@ -0,0 +1,197 @@\\n+// K116/K117 — design 275 U1 (SL-318). THE adversarial set for the ONE discovery\\n+// ledger, promoted from lead probes to a standing gate.\\n+//\\n+// The guarantee: every frame decision the coroutine transform makes is made\\n+// ONCE, recorded in the ledger, and READ from there. Before U1 roughly twenty\\n+// predicates re-derived \\\"does this callee suspend / what key names its frame /\\n+// can I build it\\\" from the raw inputs — `node.suspends`, `mangled_symbol`,\\n+// `resolved_symbol`, `module_free_call`, a callee node's written `name`, the two\\n+// suspending-method sets, both body tables, the instantiation registry — and\\n+// every one of the week's findings was two of those readings disagreeing\\n+// (SL-280's six keying sites, SL-274's two more, SL-306's classifier-versus-walk\\n+// split and its review's fourth reader). Each cell below is one of those\\n+// disagreements, arranged so the ANSWER is visible rather than inferred.\\n+//\\n+// WHY EVERY CELL ASSERTS THE IR TOO. Each of these programs prints the same\\n+// number whether or not a frame was built: a dropped suspension is a `yield_now`\\n+// that no-ops, and the value comes out right either way. `__saw_drive_steps`\\n+// counts the SUSPENSIONS the drive loop observed, so a surviving one reads 1 and\\n+// a dropped one reads 0; the `__Frame_` grep then says which frames exist — the\\n+// check the lead did by hand in three consecutive reviews, pinned here.\\n+//\\n+// The cells, and what each one would break:\\n+//\\n+//   same_name_entry / same_name_inline / same_name_imported — THREE modules\\n+//     declaring `worker`, each suspending, each answering differently. A\\n+//     bare-name key collapses them and embeds whichever body it found first, so\\n+//     the values are the oracle (+1 / +100 / +1000) and the frame set says all\\n+//     three exist.\\n+//   flat_vs_nested — a module `s_t` beside the module `s.t`, each with a\\n+//     suspending `hop`. The two render alike under a `\\\"_\\\".join` module tag\\n+//     (K110's collision) and the ledger keys them apart.\\n+//   generic_via_helper — a driven root reaching a suspending generic\\n+//     instantiation through a NON-generic helper, which is the depth the seed\\n+//     walk alone does not reach (`_promote_joining_body`'s reason for existing).\\n+//   closure_caller — a driven root whose callee suspends ONLY through the\\n+//     conservative closure-call cause (it calls `Vector.each` with a sync\\n+//     closure). Nothing is framed for it (SL-306), and the absent frames are\\n+//     the assertion: this is the cell where the classifier and the closure walk\\n+//     used to disagree.\\n+//   imported_private_by_reference — a module-private imported helper reached\\n+//     through a public entry point and taking its argument BY REFERENCE. Both\\n+//     halves matter: the private helper carries a module tag its public twin\\n+//     used not to, and the reference parameter is the body shape whose splice\\n+//     rewrites reads through `self`.\\n+//   spliced_mono_clone — an imported suspending GENERIC instantiated from the\\n+//     entry driven body. The clone is spliced into the entry module and must\\n+//     stay keyed to its HOME module, because the imported module is what emits\\n+//     the symbol.\\n+//\\n+// EXPECT: success\\n+// COMPILE-FLAGS: --module-path sl318ledger={TESTDIR}/modules/sl318ledger\\n+// EXPECT-OUTPUT:\\n+// same_name_entry 1 1\\n+// same_name_inline 100 1\\n+// same_name_imported 1000 1\\n+// flat_vs_nested 11 21\\n+// generic_via_helper 7 1\\n+// closure_caller 6 0\\n+// imported_private_by_reference 6 1\\n+// spliced_mono_clone 15 1\\n+// EXPECT-IR-CONTAINS: __Frame_worker$m$_resume\\n+// EXPECT-IR-CONTAINS: __Frame_worker$m$inner_resume\\n+// EXPECT-IR-CONTAINS: __Frame_worker$m$sl318ledger_resume\\n+// EXPECT-IR-CONTAINS: __Frame_hop$m$s_0t_resume\\n+// EXPECT-IR-CONTAINS: __Frame_hop$m$s_t_resume\\n+// EXPECT-IR-CONTAINS: __Frame_spin$m$$1$Int_resume\\n+// EXPECT-IR-CONTAINS: __Frame_bump_private$m$sl318ledger_resume\\n+// EXPECT-IR-CONTAINS: __Frame_reach_private$m$sl318ledger_resume\\n+// EXPECT-IR-CONTAINS: __Frame_warp$m$sl318ledger$1$Int_resume\\n+// EXPECT-IR-ABSENT: __Frame_fold_entry$m$\\n+\\n+import std.task.{yield_now}\\n+import sl318ledger.{Counter, reach_private, warp, worker as imported_worker}\\n+\\n+// ---------------------------------------------------------------- same name × 3\\n+\\n+/// The entry module's `worker`. +1.\\n+func worker(n: Int) -> Int {\\n+    yield_now()\\n+    n + 1\\n+}\\n+\\n+module inner {\\n+    import std.task.{yield_now}\\n+\\n+    /// An inline submodule's `worker`, one key space away from the entry\\n+    /// module's and from the dependency's. +100.\\n+    public func worker(n: Int) -> Int {\\n+        yield_now()\\n+        n + 100\\n+    }\\n+}\\n+\\n+func same_name_entry_root(n: Int) -> Int { worker(n) }\\n+func same_name_inline_root(n: Int) -> Int { inner.worker(n) }\\n+func same_name_imported_root(n: Int) -> Int { imported_worker(n) }\\n+\\n+// -------------------------------------------------------------- flat vs nested\\n+\\n+module s_t {\\n+    import std.task.{yield_now}\\n+\\n+    func hop(n: Int) -> Int {\\n+        yield_now()\\n+        n + 10\\n+    }\\n+\\n+    public func reach(n: Int) -> Int { hop(n) }\\n+}\\n+\\n+module s {\\n+    public module t {\\n+        import std.task.{yield_now}\\n+\\n+        public func hop(n: Int) -> Int {\\n+            yield_now()\\n+            n + 20\\n+        }\\n+    }\\n+}\\n+\\n+func flat_root(n: Int) -> Int { s_t.reach(n) }\\n+func nested_root(n: Int) -> Int { s.t.hop(n) }\\n+\\n+// ---------------------------------------------------------- generic via helper\\n+\\n+/// A suspending generic in the entry module. Reached through `warp_helper`, so\\n+/// the promotion has to happen as that body JOINS the driven closure rather\\n+/// than from the seed walk over the roots.\\n+func spin<T>(seed: Int) -> Int {\\n+    yield_now()\\n+    seed + 2\\n+}\\n+\\n+func warp_helper(n: Int) -> Int {\\n+    let bumped = spin<Int>(seed: n)\\n+    bumped\\n+}\\n+\\n+func generic_via_helper_root(n: Int) -> Int { warp_helper(n) }\\n+\\n+// ------------------------------------------------------------- closure caller\\n+\\n+/// Suspends ONLY by the conservative closure-call cause: `each` takes a\\n+/// non-`sync` function value, so the effect graph says \\\"might\\\" and nothing here\\n+/// owns a park. No frame is owed, at this body or at its caller.\\n+func fold_entry(v: &Vector<Int>) -> Int {\\n+    var total = 0\\n+    v.each({ [&var total] e in total = total + e })\\n+    total\\n+}\\n+\\n+func
